P208b

Summary

kb/reports/P208b.summary.txt

P208b — P208 closed. $0.264 of $1.00 (19 calls, none failed); every step-5 check passes; all thirteen papers on P206.
1 Audit-5 rule (user): compile.figure_quantity reads a user's decision on a figure label before the 0ax answer (compile.label_decision: the last user alias / symbol / supersede row of history.csv naming the paper that matches the label; collect mode still asks 0ax for every label, so the 0ax prompts do not change). New history action supersede (id the decision, target, parameters the labels, 'element: label' for one element), honoured by compile and by audit A1. Two supersede rows: yim22 'x̃₅₀%' (the round-5 alias x -> dimensionless_distance superseded by the P194 symbol of coated_aspect_ratio, as directed); ela03 'fig11a: Exposure Times' (asked in this unit: the round-5 alias -> pulse_time against your later P191 2 figure_axis decision that fig11a's series axis is feature_width [nm]; you chose P191; fig5a, fig9a, fig10a keep pulse_time). The figure labels a user decision now gives, per paper: P208b.1_figure_labels_by_decision.csv (320).
2 Audit-4 rule (user): grow.retired_by reads, of the paper's version, the line compile reads for the element (the latest answering it). yim22 had two P206 runs: its earlier pass-1 line 67 still printed the decided description labels at tabu2, the line compile reads (85) prints the symbols (\sigma_{ij}, V, \nu ...) with quantity other and answers the description cells duplicate. The 20 rows are dormant, retired by P206 (P208b.1_audit_rows.csv, with the two superseded audit-5 rows).
3 audit.py traces a P204 A3 merge (audit.merged_into: the fuller fact whose evidence lists every source); score.py runs through (exit 0 on the twelve with a gold; wer21 has no gold and is not scored).
4 mat22 and zou23 (P208b.4_pass1_mat22_zou23.csv): pass 1, whole paper, --form-version P206, the P208 prompts unchanged (6 and 1 groups from the cache, checked before and after the code change): mat22 8 calls, $0.156, 211 s; zou23 11 calls, $0.108, 139 s; no failed call. Every call of their runs parsed: P206 adopted (form_versions.csv rows, a history row). Adoption of all thirteen: P208b.4_adoption.csv.
5 Checks (P208b.5_checks.csv): the figures import of the thirteen (one at a time); compile of the thirteen; binding written 683 items with the reason 'P208: final pipeline' (P208b.5_binding_write.csv), --check 0 lost; audits 1-5 PASS; export --check 21/21; cards 0 errors; gold readability 12/12 (5357 rows); score.py P208 (kb/reports/P208.<key>.*, kb/compiled/score_summary.csv); gates.py reference: 5 of 7 rows change (P208b.5_gates.csv). Binding items whose unit changed against the committed reference: 624 (the re-extracted pass-0 units carry new ids; P208b.5_binding_moved.csv).
Per paper on the committed compile: pass-0 units by role (P208b.6_pass0_units.csv), units with no name and no distinguished_by (140), curve_per_unit panel items bound to derived units (hei24 148, yim22 74), rule-1 drops with quotes (51), rule-2 species (18), rule-3 transform rows against the same compile without rule 3 (24), no_knowledge on numeric elements: 0.
Matched gold (P208b.6_gold_matched.csv; before = the P201 A compile): 2576 -> 2452. Gains: yim22 341 -> 370, mat23 85 -> 96, gao15 117 -> 126, gre23 114 -> 122, gor03 61 -> 66, zou23 71 -> 77. Falls: mat22 452 -> 292 (activity 127 -> 32, entity 44 -> 10, observation 115 -> 91: its new pass-0 runs no longer pair with the gold runs, 36 element_ratio observations lost, 131 records dropped for a quote not found; mat22 is now the gates' worst 'quotes not found' share, 0.289), ela03 172 -> 155, yim20 524 -> 512, hei24 391 -> 389, art19 99 -> 98; yli18 unchanged. Every row lost (445) or gained (321), with what its elements hold now, for your veto by name: P208b.6_gold_changes.csv.
